从系统发育树预测病毒 进化

Forecasting viral evolution from phylogenetic trees
作者： Ivan Specht, Soyoon Park, Seyone Chithrananda, Driscoll CL, Garyk Brixi, Julia A. Palacios, Brian Hie
摘要中文翻译病毒 突变 预测在大流行防范中发挥着关键作用,使研究人员能够预测新的变异并设计主动干预措施。以系统发育树为代表的进化历史为过去和现在菌株的出现提供了重要的见解,但它们在预测未来序列变化方面的作用在很大程度上仍未被探索。我们引入了 antiGen,这是一种机器学习模型,可以通过学习病毒的进化历史来预测病毒的进化未来。antiGen 在预测 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白的 突变 方面实现了最先进的性能,预测了模型训练窗口数年后出现的前所未见的 突变 和 突变。antiGen 预测的尖峰 突变 在体外也保留了假病毒感染性。此外,尽管可用的测序数据少得多,但 antiGen 对流感病毒、呼吸道合胞病毒 和登革热病毒的表面蛋白表现出领先的预测性能。明确学习系统发育结构的病毒 进化 模型为从流行病学建模到治疗开发等应用提供了宝贵的资源。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决理解如何使用系统发育树来预测未来病毒 突变 方面的差距,这对于大流行防范和主动干预设计至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究引入了 antiGen,这是一种机器学习模型,旨在通过分析病毒 进化 的进化历史来预测病毒 进化,重点关注 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 和相关病毒。
核心方法
antiGen 是使用机器学习技术开发的,可根据病毒表面蛋白的系统发育树预测 呼吸道合胞病毒、流感病毒和登革热病毒的表面蛋白中的 突变。
主要结果
antiGen 在预测 SARS-CoV-2 刺突蛋白的 突变 方面表现出了最先进的性能,包括在训练期间未观察到的新型和长期 突变。
解释与创新
该模型预测 突变 并在体外保留假病毒感染性的能力凸显了其在理解病毒 进化 和设计干预措施方面的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为流行病学建模和治疗开发提供了宝贵的工具,特别是对于 呼吸道合胞病毒 和相关病原体。
局限与证据强度
该研究的仅抽象证据限制了分析的深度,但该模型在有限数据上的表现表明对其预测能力具有中等程度的信心。

Forecasting viral evolution from phylogenetic trees

Authors: Ivan Specht, Soyoon Park, Seyone Chithrananda, Driscoll CL, Garyk Brixi, Julia A. Palacios, Brian Hie
Original abstract (English)
Viral mutation forecasting plays a key role in pandemic preparedness by enabling researchers to anticipate novel variants and design proactive interventions. Evolutionary histories, represented as phylogenetic trees, offer key insights into the emergence of past and present strains, yet their role in predicting future sequence changes remains largely unexplored. We introduce antiGen, a machine learning model that forecasts the evolutionary future of viruses by learning from their evolutionary past. antiGen achieves state-of-the-art performance for predicting mutations to the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein, anticipating never-before-seen mutations and mutations that emerge years after the model's training window. antiGen-forecasted spike mutations also retain pseudoviral infectivity in vitro. Moreover, antiGen demonstrates leading predictive performance on surface proteins of influenza virus, respiratory syncytial virus, and dengue virus despite far less available sequencing data. Viral evolution models that explicitly learn from phylogenetic structure offer a valuable resource for applications ranging from epidemiological modeling to therapeutic development.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the gap in understanding how phylogenetic trees can be used to predict future viral mutations, which is critical for pandemic preparedness and proactive intervention design.
Design and population
The study introduced antiGen, a machine learning model designed to forecast viral evolution by analyzing their evolutionary past, with a focus on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) and related viruses.
Core methods
antiGen was developed using machine learning techniques to forecast mutations in viral surface proteins, including those of respiratory syncytial virus, influenza virus, and dengue virus, based on their phylogenetic trees.
Main results
antiGen demonstrated state-of-the-art performance in predicting mutations to the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein, including novel and long-term mutations not observed during its training period.
Interpretation and novelty
The model's ability to forecast mutations and retain pseudoviral infectivity in vitro highlights its potential for use in understanding viral evolution and designing interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a valuable tool for epidemiological modeling and therapeutic development, particularly for respiratory syncytial virus and related pathogens.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's abstract-only evidence limits the depth of analysis, but the model's performance on limited data suggests a moderate level of confidence in its predictive capabilities.

针对癌症患者的现场 疫苗接种 活动的有效性。

Effectiveness of an on-site vaccination campaign for patients with cancer.
作者： Theresa Markus, Sarah Eitze, Ruth Flümann, Philipp Gödel, Michael Hallek, Sebastian Herrmann, Udo Holtick, Jonathan Köllges, Petra Langerbeins, Janina Leckler
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估和增强癌症患者对 疫苗 的吸收,因为他们患严重感染的风险增加。疫苗接种 是一项关键的预防策略,由国家免疫技术咨询组 (NITAG) 推荐。
设计与对象
德国科隆大学医院进行了一项前后观察研究,以评估 1.5 年干预期间的 疫苗接种 状态。相关感染包括COVID-19、流感、带状疱疹、肺炎球菌和呼吸道合胞病毒。总共纳入 749 名患者(2023 年 n = 398 名,2025 年 n = 351 名)。
核心方法
该活动包括患者教育、员工培训、现场疫苗接种以及有针对性的宣传工作。研究设计涉及评估干预前后的 疫苗接种 状态。
主要结果
Overall vaccination rates increased from 43 to 49%, while COVID-19 vaccine coverage remained above 90%. Notable increases were observed for influenza (37% to 48%), pneumococcal (16% to 36%), and herpes zoster (22% to 33%) vaccines.
解释与创新
有针对性的 疫苗接种 活动可以有效增加癌症患者对 疫苗 的摄取;然而,按照建议的时间表接种疫苗的个人比例仍然很低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,有针对性的干预措施可以提高癌症患者的 疫苗接种 率,这对于降低严重感染的风险非常重要。未来的研究应该调查 疫苗 拒绝指导更有针对性的干预措施的障碍和原因。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象分析,证据基于观察数据,没有详细的统计分析。由于缺乏全面的数据,研究结果的可信度较低。

Effectiveness of an on-site vaccination campaign for patients with cancer.

Authors: Theresa Markus, Sarah Eitze, Ruth Flümann, Philipp Gödel, Michael Hallek, Sebastian Herrmann, Udo Holtick, Jonathan Köllges, Petra Langerbeins, Janina Leckler
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Patients with haematological or oncological malignancies are at increased risk of severe infections. Vaccination is a key preventive strategy and is recommended by National Immunization Technical Advisory Groups (NITAGs). Yet, vaccination rates in this population remain improvable. This project aimed to evaluate and enhance vaccine uptake in cancer patients. Methods We conducted a pre- and post-observational study at the University Hospital Cologne in Germany to assess vaccination status over a 1.5-year intervention. Relevant infections included COVID-19, influenza, herpes zoster, pneumococci and RSV. The campaign included patient education, staff training, on-site vaccination, and targeted promotional efforts. Results A total of 749 patients were included (n = 398 in 2023, n = 351 in 2025). Overall vaccination rates increased from 43 to 49%, while COVID-19 vaccine coverage remained above 90%. Notable increases were observed for influenza (37% to 48%), pneumococcal (16% to 36%), and herpes zoster (22% to 33%) vaccines. The proportion of patients fully vaccinated according to public health recommendations rose from 6 to 16%, whereas those completely unvaccinated remained low (4% to 6%). Conclusion Focused vaccination campaigns can effectively increase vaccine uptake in patients with cancer; however, the proportion of individuals vaccinated per recommended schedule remains low. Future research should investigate barriers and reasons for vaccine refusal to guide more targeted interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate and enhance vaccine uptake in cancer patients, as they are at increased risk of severe infections. Vaccination is a key preventive strategy and is recommended by National Immunization Technical Advisory Groups (NITAGs).
Design and population
A pre- and post-observational study was conducted at the University Hospital Cologne in Germany to assess vaccination status over a 1.5-year intervention. Relevant infections included COVID-19, influenza, herpes zoster, pneumococci and RSV. A total of 749 patients were included (n = 398 in 2023, n = 351 in 2025).
Core methods
The campaign included patient education, staff training, on-site vaccination, and targeted promotional efforts. The study design involved assessing vaccination status before and after the intervention.
Main results
Overall vaccination rates increased from 43 to 49%, while COVID-19 vaccine coverage remained above 90%. Notable increases were observed for influenza (37% to 48%), pneumococcal (16% to 36%), and herpes zoster (22% to 33%) vaccines.
Interpretation and novelty
Focused vaccination campaigns can effectively increase vaccine uptake in patients with cancer; however, the proportion of individuals vaccinated per recommended schedule remains low.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeted interventions can improve vaccination rates in cancer patients, which is important for reducing the risk of severe infections. Future research should investigate barriers and reasons for vaccine refusal to guide more targeted interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only analysis, and the evidence is based on observational data without detailed statistical analysis. The confidence in the findings is moderate due to the lack of comprehensive data.

尼塞韦单抗 针对 RSV-LRTI 和 毛细支气管炎 住院治疗的实际有效性:2024-2025 年意大利新生儿全民免疫运动的结果

Real-World Effectiveness of Nirsevimab Against RSV-LRTI and Bronchiolitis Hospitalization: Results from the 2024–2025 Italian Universal Immunization Campaign in Newborns
作者： Cinzia Auriti, Camilla Gizzi, Matteo Riccò, Roberto Bellù, Mario Giuffrè, Adele Fabiano, Caterina Cacace, Paola Cavicchioli, Armando Cuttano, Susanna Di Valerio
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 仍然是婴儿早期下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 和住院的主要原因。该研究旨在评估 尼塞韦单抗 预防 RSV 相关住院治疗和严重临床结果的现实效果。
设计与对象
Following the introduction of universal nirsevimab prophylaxis in Italy during the 2024–2025 RSV season, a multicenter retrospective test-negative study was conducted across 88 Italian neonatal centers, including infants hospitalized for LRTI from 01/09/2024 to 30/04/2025. RSV-positive hospitalized infants were considered cases, and RSV-negative hospitalized infants served as controls.
核心方法
使用标准化电子问卷从医院记录中收集临床数据。使用多变量逻辑回归和目标最大似然估计来估计有效性。
主要结果
总体而言,共纳入 737 名婴儿,其中 306 名(41.52%)曾接受过 尼塞韦单抗。在 398 名婴儿 (54.00%) 中发现 RSV 感染,其中包括 104/306 (33.99%) 尼塞韦单抗 免疫婴儿和 294/431 (68.21%) 未免疫婴儿 (p < 0.001)。免疫婴儿需要呼吸支持的频率也较低(55.23% vs. 70.07%;p < 0.001),高流量氧疗(40.85% vs. 48.96%;p = 0.036)和持续气道正压通气(CPAP)(18.95% vs.
解释与创新
在测试阴性设计框架内,针对 RSV 相关住院治疗的调整后有效性估计为 78.3%。之前的 尼塞韦单抗 免疫还与调整后的呼吸支持和 NICU 入住率相对降低相关,分别为 80.2% 和 64.3%。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现进一步证明了 尼塞韦单抗 对严重 RSV 疾病的现实有效性,这可能为未来 疫苗接种 新生儿群体 RSV 预防策略和公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管这些发现进一步证明了 发病率 对严重 RSV 疾病的现实有效性,但仍需要进一步的研究来提供 发病率 在免疫和非免疫源人群中住院治疗的直接估计。

Real-World Effectiveness of Nirsevimab Against RSV-LRTI and Bronchiolitis Hospitalization: Results from the 2024–2025 Italian Universal Immunization Campaign in Newborns

Authors: Cinzia Auriti, Camilla Gizzi, Matteo Riccò, Roberto Bellù, Mario Giuffrè, Adele Fabiano, Caterina Cacace, Paola Cavicchioli, Armando Cuttano, Susanna Di Valerio
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) remains a leading cause of lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) and hospitalization in early infancy. Following the introduction of universal nirsevimab prophylaxis in Italy during the 2024–2025 RSV season, we evaluated its real-world effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalization and severe clinical outcomes within a test-negative design framework. We conducted a multicenter retrospective test-negative study across 88 Italian neonatal centers, including infants hospitalized for LRTI from 01/09/2024 to 30/04/2025. Clinical data were collected from hospital records using a standardized electronic questionnaire. RSV-positive hospitalized infants were considered cases, and RSV-negative hospitalized infants served as controls. Secondary outcomes included respiratory support and Neonatal Intensive Care Unit (NICU) admission. Effectiveness was estimated using multivariable logistic regression and targeted maximum likelihood estimation. Overall, 737 infants were included, of whom 306 (41.52%) had previously received nirsevimab. RSV infection was identified in 398 infants (54.00%), including 104/306 (33.99%) nirsevimab-immunized and 294/431 (68.21%) non-immunized infants (p < 0.001). Immunized infants also required respiratory support less frequently (55.23% vs. 70.07%; p < 0.001), with lower use of high-flow oxygen therapy (40.85% vs. 48.96%; p = 0.036) and Continuous Positive Airway Pressure (CPAP) (18.95% vs. Within the test-negative design framework, adjusted effectiveness against RSV-associated hospitalization was estimated at 78.3%. Among hospitalized infants, previous nirsevimab immunization was additionally associated with adjusted relative reductions in the odds of respiratory support and NICU admission of 80.2% and 64.3%, respectively. In conclusion, among infants hospitalized for LRTI in routine Italian clinical practice, previous nirsevimab immunization was associated with lower odds of RSV positivity and a reduced need for respiratory support. Although these findings provide further evidence of the real-world effectiveness of nirsevimab against severe RSV disease, further studies are needed to provide direct estimates of hospitalization incidence in immunized and non-immunized source populations.
View research seven-element analysis

Interpretation and novelty

Previous nirsevimab immunization was additionally associated with adjusted relative reductions in the odds of respiratory support and NICU admission of 80.2% and 64.3%, respectively.

使用明胶膜作为采样基质,验证标准化定量 RT-qPCR 工作流程,用于检测和定量空气传播的呼吸道病毒。

Validation of a standardized quantitative RT-qPCR workflow for detection and quantification of airborne respiratory viruses using gelatin membranes as sampling matrix.
作者： Irene Tellini Rusticano, Fabiola Berti, Carmela Calonico, Sara Boccalini, Angela Bechini
摘要中文翻译空气微生物监测对于识别和量化病毒病原体越来越重要;然而,从环境基质中回收的呼吸道病毒的标准化分析工作流程仍然有限。本研究报告了定量 RT-qPCR 工作流程的全面分析验证,用于从明胶膜过滤器收集的空气样本中同时检测和定量 SARS-CoV-2、甲型流感、乙型流感和 RSV A/B。该方案优化了这种具有挑战性的基质中的 RNA 提取和扩增,并提供了适合可重复实验室实施的标准化程序。所有验证参数(扩增效率、线性度、回收率、基质干扰、检测限 (LOD)、定量限 (LOQ)、重复性和再现性)均按照 ISO 20395 和 MIQE 指南进行评估,并满足所有预定义的验收标准。该工作流程显示出出色的线性度(所有目标的 R 2 ≥ 0.99)、高扩增效率(92-103%)、稳健的回收率(81.98-92.28%),并且没有明显的基质干扰(±10%)。LOD 范围为 5 至 9 个拷贝/反应,LOQ 范围为 8 至 10.5 个拷贝/反应。精度评估表明测试内和测试间变异性可接受(CV% ≤ 35%,SD < 0.19)。总体而言,这种经过验证且符合标准的 RT-qPCR 工作流程为实验室定量明胶膜过滤器上收集的空气传播呼吸道病毒提供了可靠、灵敏且可重复的分析工具,支持气溶胶样品的标准化病毒学分析。

Validation of a standardized quantitative RT-qPCR workflow for detection and quantification of airborne respiratory viruses using gelatin membranes as sampling matrix.

Authors: Irene Tellini Rusticano, Fabiola Berti, Carmela Calonico, Sara Boccalini, Angela Bechini
Original abstract (English)
Airborne microbiological monitoring is increasingly relevant for identifying and quantifying viral pathogens; however, standardized analytical workflows for respiratory viruses recovered from environmental matrices remain limited. This study reports the full analytical validation of a quantitative RT-qPCR workflow for the simultaneous detection and quantification of SARS-CoV-2, influenza A, influenza B, and RSV A/B from air samples collected on gelatin membrane filters. The protocol optimizes RNA extraction and amplification from this challenging matrix and provides a standardized procedure suitable for reproducible laboratory implementation. All validation parameters-amplification efficiency, linearity, recovery, matrix interference, limit of detection (LOD), limit of quantification (LOQ), repeatability, and reproducibility-were assessed in compliance with ISO 20395 and MIQE guidelines and met all predefined acceptance criteria. The workflow showed excellent linearity (R 2 ≥ 0.99 for all targets), high amplification efficiency (92-103%), robust recovery (81.98-92.28%), and no significant matrix interference (±10%). LODs ranged from 5 to 9 copies/reaction, and LOQs from 8 to 10.5 copies/reaction. Precision assessments demonstrated acceptable intra- and inter-test variability (CV% ≤ 35%, SD < 0.19). Overall, this validated and standards-compliant RT-qPCR workflow provides a reliable, sensitive, and reproducible analytical tool for laboratory quantification of airborne respiratory viruses collected on gelatin membrane filters, supporting standardized virological analysis of aerosol samples.

通过纳米乳胶靶向输送瑞舒伐他汀:迈向更有效的类风湿性关节炎治疗的一步。

Targeted drug delivery of rosuvastatin via nanoemulgel: A step towards more effective rheumatoid arthritis therapy.
作者： Vrushali Pathade, Shweta Nene, Shreya Ratnam, Kamatham Pushpa Tryphena, Etikala Amulya, Ganesh Vambhurkar, Saurabh Srivastava
摘要中文翻译当前研究的目的是重新利用瑞舒伐他汀(RSV)(一种抗高血脂药物)来治疗类风湿性关节炎(RA)。RSV 是一种重新利用的药物,装载在纳米乳胶 (NEG) 中,经皮递送可改善药物通过皮肤的渗透并到达所需的作用部位。因此,这提高了药物在作用部位的浓度,减少了与其他给药途径相关的副作用,进一步显示了在 RA 治疗中的抗炎潜力。采用自发乳化法制备纳米乳液(NE)。通过 Malvern ZetaSizer 评估了所开发的 NE 的球体尺寸和多分散指数。此外,RSV-NEG 已得到开发和表征。在完全弗氏佐剂诱导的大鼠 RA 中评估了 RSV-NEG 的体内功效。通过酶联免疫吸附测定对大鼠血清中促炎细胞因子浓度进行定量。0.4% w/w RSV NE 的小球尺寸和多分散指数分别为 8.96 ± 0.19 nm 和 < 0.3。开发的 NEG 显示出假塑性剪切稀化行为,平均药物含量为 97.54 ± 0.36%。与游离 RSV 凝胶相比,RSV-NEG 的 RSV 渗透率高 28 倍。与阴性对照组相比,用 RSV-NEG 治疗的动物表现出抗炎潜力,同时爪子厚度和关节炎评分显着降低 (****p < 0.0001)。局部应用后的渗透性和抗炎潜力的有希望的发现强调了 RSV-NEG 有效管理 RA 的能力。

问题与背景
RSV 是一种重新利用的药物,装载在纳米乳胶 (NEG) 中,经皮递送可改善药物通过皮肤的渗透并到达所需的作用部位。
设计与对象
当前研究的目的是重新利用瑞舒伐他汀(RSV)(一种抗高血脂药物)来治疗类风湿性关节炎(RA)。
核心方法
通过酶联免疫吸附测定对大鼠血清中促炎细胞因子浓度进行定量。
主要结果

Targeted drug delivery of rosuvastatin via nanoemulgel: A step towards more effective rheumatoid arthritis therapy.

Authors: Vrushali Pathade, Shweta Nene, Shreya Ratnam, Kamatham Pushpa Tryphena, Etikala Amulya, Ganesh Vambhurkar, Saurabh Srivastava
Original abstract (English)
The intent of the current study was the repurposing of rosuvastatin (RSV), an anti-hyperlipidemic drug, for the management of rheumatoid arthritis (RA). Transdermal delivery of RSV, a repurposed drug, loaded in nanoemulgel (NEG) improves the permeation of the drug through the skin and reaches the desired site of action. This thereby enhances the concentration of the drug at the site of action, reducing the side effects associated with other routes of delivery, further showing anti-inflammatory potential in the management of RA. Nanoemulsion (NE) was prepared by the spontaneous emulsification method. The globule size and polydispersity index of the developed NE were evaluated by Malvern ZetaSizer. Furthermore, RSV-NEG has been developed and characterized. In vivo efficacy of RSV-NEG was evaluated in complete Freund's adjuvant-induced RA in rats. Pro-inflammatory cytokine concentrations in rat serum were quantified by enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay. The globule size and polydispersity index of 0.4% w/w RSV NE were found to be 8.96 ± 0.19 nm and < 0.3, respectively. The developed NEG showed pseudoplastic shear thinning behavior and an average drug content of 97.54 ± 0.36%. The RSV demonstrated 28-fold higher permeation from RSV-NEG compared to free RSV gel. The animals treated with RSV-NEG exhibited anti-inflammatory potential along with significantly reduced (****p < 0.0001) paw thickness and arthritis scores compared to the negative control group. The promising findings with permeability after topical application and anti-inflammatory potential emphasize the ability of RSV-NEG to manage RA effectively.

Transdermal delivery of RSV, a repurposed drug, loaded in nanoemulgel (NEG) improves the permeation of the drug through the skin and reaches the desired site of action.
Design and population

Core methods

Main results
The developed NEG showed pseudoplastic shear thinning behavior and an average drug content of 97.54 ± 0.36%.
Interpretation and novelty

Scientific and public-health significance
The animals treated with RSV-NEG exhibited anti-inflammatory potential along with significantly reduced (****p < 0.0001) paw thickness and arthritis scores compared to the negative control group.
Limitations and evidence strength
The promising findings with permeability after topical application and anti-inflammatory potential emphasize the ability of RSV-NEG to manage RA effectively.

香港孕妇和老年人接受 RSV 疫苗 的障碍和促进因素:以理论领域框架为指导的定性研究

Barriers and facilitators to RSV vaccine acceptance among pregnant women and older adults in Hong Kong: a qualitative study guided by the Theoretical Domains Framework
作者： Claire Chenwen Zhong, Man Kin Yim, Zehuan Yang, Vera MW Keung, Chung Yi Lo, Xiaoshu Zhang, Mingtao Chen, Junjie Huang, Martin CS Wong
摘要中文翻译摘要 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 对孕妇、婴儿和老年人构成重大健康风险,但全球范围内 疫苗 的吸收率仍然不理想。本研究旨在探讨影响香港孕妇和老年人接受 RSV 疫苗 的障碍和促进因素。采用定性设计,对香港的孕妇和老年人进行半结构化访谈。访谈指南以理论领域框架 (TDF) 为基础,捕捉 14 个领域的行为决定因素。使用主题分析对数据进行分析,将基于 TDF 域的演绎编码与新兴主题的归纳识别相结合。障碍和促进因素被进一步分类以方便解释。在本研究纳入的 24 名参与者中,9 人表示愿意接受 RSV 疫苗接种,而 15 人表示不愿意。常见的障碍包括财务问题、对潜在副作用的担忧以及对 RSV 及其 疫苗 的了解有限。对医疗保健专业人员和政府当局的信任被普遍认为是一个促进因素。其他促进因素包括费用减免或补贴、获得更全面的 疫苗 信息以及社区成员、家人和朋友的影响。知识差距、安全问题、财务考虑和社会影响是 RSV 疫苗接种 决策中反复出现的主题,而对医疗保健提供者和政府当局的信任是一个显着的促进因素。未来的研究应该应用行为框架来设计和评估可以在香港医疗保健系统内推广的有针对性的干预措施。

设计与对象
其他促进因素包括费用减免或补贴、获得更全面的 疫苗 信息以及社区成员、家人和朋友的影响。

Barriers and facilitators to RSV vaccine acceptance among pregnant women and older adults in Hong Kong: a qualitative study guided by the Theoretical Domains Framework

Authors: Claire Chenwen Zhong, Man Kin Yim, Zehuan Yang, Vera MW Keung, Chung Yi Lo, Xiaoshu Zhang, Mingtao Chen, Junjie Huang, Martin CS Wong
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) poses a significant health risk to pregnant women, infants, and older adults, yet vaccine uptake remains suboptimal globally. This study aimed to explore the barriers and facilitators influencing RSV vaccine acceptance among pregnant women and older adults in Hong Kong. A qualitative design was employed, involving semi-structured interviews with pregnant women and older adults in Hong Kong. The interview guide was informed by the Theoretical Domains Framework (TDF) to capture behavioural determinants across 14 domains. Data were analyzed using thematic analysis, combining deductive coding based on TDF domains with inductive identification of emergent themes. Barriers and facilitators were further categorized to facilitate interpretation. Of the 24 participants included in this study, 9 expressed willingness to receive RSV vaccination, while 15 expressed unwillingness. Commonly reported barriers included financial concerns, worries about potential side effects, and limited knowledge about RSV and its vaccines. Trust in healthcare professionals and government authorities was commonly recognized as a facilitator. Additional facilitators included the availability of fee waivers or subsidies, access to more comprehensive vaccine information, and influence from community members, family, and friends. Knowledge gaps, safety concerns, financial considerations, and social influence were recurring themes in RSV vaccination decision-making, while trust in healthcare providers and government authorities was a salient facilitator. Future research should apply behavioural frameworks to design and evaluate targeted interventions that can be scaled within Hong Kong’s healthcare system.
View research seven-element analysis

Abstract Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) poses a significant health risk to pregnant women, infants, and older adults, yet vaccine uptake remains suboptimal globally.
Design and population
Additional facilitators included the availability of fee waivers or subsidies, access to more comprehensive vaccine information, and influence from community members, family, and friends.
Core methods

Main results

Interpretation and novelty

Limitations and evidence strength

流感与 呼吸道合胞病毒 之间的相互作用:分析多重数据的两种病原体模型

Interactions between influenza and respiratory syncytial viruses: two-pathogen modelling to analyse multiplex data
作者： George Shirreff, Sandra S. Chaves, Laurent Coudeville, Beatriz Mengual-Chuliá, Ainara Mira-Iglesias, Joan Puig-Barberà, Alejandro Orrico-Sanchez, Javier Díez-Domingo, Lulla Opatowski, F. Xavier Lopez-Labrador
摘要中文翻译背景:流感和 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 都会导致年度住院高峰,并且有相当大的重叠。流行病学研究表明存在抑制关系,这一点得到了实验室研究的支持。已经确定了包括资源竞争和免疫激活在内的几种生物机制,但区分其在人群水平上的影响的证据有限。方法:我们开发了一种流感-RSV 区室模型,利用 2010 年至 2020 年西班牙巴伦西亚病毒相关住院数据来估计和比较潜在的相互作用。我们考虑了五种相互作用机制:易感性、传播性和严重性改变、感染后不应期和残留阳性期。使用贝叶斯推理,我们将模型与流感、RSV 或两者兼而有之的每周住院情况以及流感样疾病的国家 监测 数据进行拟合。结果:我们发现了流感和 RSV 之间抑制的证据,并支持了几种相互作用机制。在最佳拟合模型中,活动性 RSV 感染将因流感住院的概率降低至基线的 20%(数据支持值为 0%-60%)。与没有相互作用的模型相比,这种机制使流感-RSV 联合检测的医院病例数减少了 63%。结论:我们的结果支持抑制机制的存在,对疾病 流行病学 有微小但实质性的影响。这些结果凸显了在呼吸道病毒和 疫苗接种 不断变化的情况下了解这些相互作用的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨流感与呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)的相互作用,因为两者均会导致医院住院率的年度高峰,并且存在显著的重叠。目前在群体层面区分其影响的证据有限。
设计与对象
该研究根据 2010 年至 2020 年西班牙巴伦西亚的数据,使用双病原体区室模型来估计流感和 RSV 之间的相互作用。其中包括与流感、RSV 或两者相关的住院治疗。
核心方法
研究人员开发了流感-RSV 区室模型来估计和比较潜在的相互作用。他们考虑了五种相互作用机制:易感性、传播性和严重性改变、感染后不应期和残留阳性期。
主要结果
最佳拟合模型显示,活动性 RSV 感染将因流感住院的可能性降低至基线的 20%。这一机制使流感-RSV 联合检测的医院病例数减少了 63%。
解释与创新
结果支持流感和 RSV 之间存在抑制机制,对疾病 流行病学 具有微小但实质性的影响。这些发现强调了了解呼吸道病毒和 流行病学 不断变化的情况下这些相互作用的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为 监测 和呼吸道病毒的风险评估策略提供信息,特别是在了解病原体之间的相互作用如何影响疾病负担和住院率方面。
局限与证据强度
该研究使用了适合每周住院治疗和国家 监测 数据的模型,这可能会在捕捉复杂的相互作用时引入偏差或限制。证据是中等的,因为它依赖于基于模型的推理而不是直接的实验验证。

Interactions between influenza and respiratory syncytial viruses: two-pathogen modelling to analyse multiplex data

Authors: George Shirreff, Sandra S. Chaves, Laurent Coudeville, Beatriz Mengual-Chuliá, Ainara Mira-Iglesias, Joan Puig-Barberà, Alejandro Orrico-Sanchez, Javier Díez-Domingo, Lulla Opatowski, F. Xavier Lopez-Labrador
Original abstract (English)
Background: Influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) both cause annual peaks in hospitalisation and with considerable overlap. Epidemiological studies suggest an inhibitory relationship, which is supported by laboratory studies. Several biological mechanisms including resource competition and immune activation have been identified, but limited evidence exists to distinguish their effects at the population level. Methods: We developed an influenza-RSV compartmental model to estimate and compare potential interactions using data on virus-associated hospitalisations in Valencia, Spain from 2010 through 2020. We considered five interaction mechanisms: altered susceptibility, transmissibility and severity, a refractory period post-infection, and a residual positivity period. Using Bayesian inference, we fit the model to weekly hospitalisations with influenza, RSV or both, and to national surveillance data for influenza-like illness. Results: We found evidence for inhibition between influenza and RSV, with several interaction mechanisms being supported. In the best-fitting model, active RSV infection reduced the probability of hospitalisation with influenza to 20% of the baseline (with the data supporting values from 0%-60%). Compared to a model without interaction, this mechanism reduced the number of hospital cases with influenza-RSV co-detection by 63%. Conclusions: Our results support the existence of inhibitory mechanisms, with small but substantial effects on disease epidemiology. These results highlight the importance of understanding these interactions under the changing epidemiology of respiratory viruses and vaccinations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the interactions between influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), as both cause annual peaks in hospitalisation with considerable overlap. Limited evidence exists to distinguish their effects at the population level.
Design and population
The study used a two-pathogen compartmental model to estimate interactions between influenza and RSV, based on data from Valencia, Spain, spanning 2010 through 2020. It included hospitalisations associated with influenza, RSV, or both.
Core methods
The researchers developed an influenza-RSV compartmental model to estimate and compare potential interactions. They considered five interaction mechanisms: altered susceptibility, transmissibility and severity, a refractory period post-infection, and a residual positivity period.
Main results
The best-fitting model showed that active RSV infection reduced the probability of hospitalisation with influenza to 20% of the baseline. This mechanism reduced the number of hospital cases with influenza-RSV co-detection by 63%.
Interpretation and novelty
The results support the existence of inhibitory mechanisms between influenza and RSV, with small but substantial effects on disease epidemiology. These findings highlight the importance of understanding these interactions under the changing epidemiology of respiratory viruses and vaccinations.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance and risk assessment strategies for respiratory viruses, particularly in understanding how interactions between pathogens may influence disease burden and hospitalisation rates.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study used a model fitted to weekly hospitalisations and national surveillance data, which may introduce biases or limitations in capturing complex interactions. The evidence is moderate, as it relies on model-based inference rather than direct experimental validation.

使用 毛细支气管炎 对住院婴儿进行呼吸道病原体联合检测并实施通用 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防:一项为期六年的观察性研究

Respiratory Pathogen Co-Detection in Hospitalized Infants with Bronchiolitis and Implementation of Universal Nirsevimab Immunoprophylaxis: A Six-Year Observational Study
作者： Gregorio Serra, Chiara Martorana, Giovanni Barbera, Mariavalentina Catania, Domenico Cipolla, Claudia Colomba, Piero Farruggia, Mario Giuffrè, Giulia La Malfa, Martina Greco
摘要中文翻译背景:毛细支气管炎 是婴儿住院的主要原因,其中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是主要病原体。呼吸道病原体合并感染的临床相关性以及包括 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防在内的新兴预防策略的作用仍在研究中。方法:我们在西西里岛西部的儿科医疗保健网络中进行了两项补充观察分析。第一项是一项回顾性研究,纳入了 2019 年至 2025 年间在意大利巴勒莫“Giovanni Di Cristina”医院因 毛细支气管炎 住院的 580 名 24 个月以下儿童,以调查呼吸道病原体检测模式及其临床相关性。根据微生物学结果将患者分类为单一病毒感染或病毒-病毒/病毒-细菌联合检测。第二个是 2024-2025 年 RSV 季节期间对来自西西里岛西部两个出生中心的 458 名新生儿进行的单独观察性 队列研究,旨在评估与 尼塞韦单抗 预防相关的实施、安全性和呼吸结果。结果:45.5% 的住院婴儿中发现了病毒-病毒和/或病毒-细菌联合检测,并且与较长的住院时间(8.86 天 vs. 6.35 天,p < 0.001)和较高的并发症发生率(18.6% vs. 8.5%,p < 0.001)相关,且年龄、性别或呼吸支持需求没有差异。在整个研究期间,RSV 仍然是主要病原体。在补充观察亚组分析中,观察到的安全性结果令人放心,在随访期间仅报告了轻度和自限性事件;然而,样本量和观察设计限制了评估罕见或延迟不良事件的能力。在接受临床指示呼吸道检测的婴儿中,7/9 (77.8%) 未预防婴儿和 5/24 (20.8%) 预防婴儿中检测到 RSV。在住院婴儿中,接受 尼塞韦单抗 治疗的婴儿出现并发症的频率较低(3.5% vs. 26.9%,p < 0.001),尽管各组之间的基线差异显着,无法对这一发现进行因果解释。结论:病毒-病毒和病毒-细菌联合检测在住院 毛细支气管炎 中很常见,并且与临床复杂性增加相关,主要是通过住院时间延长和并发症发生率更高。观察性 尼塞韦单抗 分析显示,在临床测试的预防婴儿中,安全性良好,且 RSV 检出比例较低。然而,由于呼吸测试是根据临床指征进行的,并且组间差异很大,因此这些发现不能解释为 RSV 发病率 的估计或 发病率 有效性的明确证据。由于预防和未预防婴儿之间存在显着的基线差异,因此也应谨慎解释观察到的并发症差异。有必要进行系统呼吸测试的前瞻性研究。

问题与背景
毛细支气管炎 是婴儿住院的主要原因,其中 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是主要病原体。呼吸道病原体合并感染的临床相关性以及包括 尼塞韦单抗 免疫预防在内的新兴预防策略的作用仍在研究中。
设计与对象
我们在西西里岛西部的儿科医疗保健网络中进行了两项补充观察分析。第一项是一项回顾性研究,纳入了 2019 年至 2025 年间在意大利巴勒莫“Giovanni Di Cristina”医院因 毛细支气管炎 住院的 580 名 24 个月以下儿童。第二个是 2024-2025 年 RSV 季节期间对来自西西里岛西部两个出生中心的 458 名新生儿进行的单独观察 队列研究。

解释与创新
病毒-病毒和病毒-细菌联合检测在住院 毛细支气管炎 中很常见,并且与临床复杂性增加相关,主要是由于住院时间延长和并发症发生率更高。观察性 尼塞韦单抗 分析显示,在临床测试的预防婴儿中,安全性良好,且 RSV 检出比例较低。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,呼吸道病原体的合并感染增加了 毛细支气管炎 婴儿的临床复杂性,突出表明需要改进 毛细支气管炎 和有针对性的干预措施。观察到的 尼塞韦单抗 的安全性可能为未来在高危人群中实施的策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于预防和未预防婴儿之间存在显着的基线差异,因此也应谨慎解释观察到的并发症差异。观察设计和样本量限制了评估罕见或延迟不良事件的能力。

Respiratory Pathogen Co-Detection in Hospitalized Infants with Bronchiolitis and Implementation of Universal Nirsevimab Immunoprophylaxis: A Six-Year Observational Study

Authors: Gregorio Serra, Chiara Martorana, Giovanni Barbera, Mariavalentina Catania, Domenico Cipolla, Claudia Colomba, Piero Farruggia, Mario Giuffrè, Giulia La Malfa, Martina Greco
Original abstract (English)
Background: Bronchiolitis is a leading cause of hospitalization in infants, with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) as the main etiological agent. The clinical relevance of respiratory pathogen co-infections and the role of emerging preventive strategies, including nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis, remain under investigation. Methods: We conducted two complementary observational analyses within the pediatric healthcare network of Western Sicily. The first was a retrospective study including 580 children younger than 24 months hospitalized for bronchiolitis between 2019 and 2025 at the “Giovanni Di Cristina” Hospital in Palermo, Italy, to investigate respiratory pathogen detection patterns and their clinical correlates. Patients were classified according to microbiological findings as single-virus infections or viral–viral/viral–bacterial co-detections. The second was a separate observational cohort of 458 newborns from two birth centers in Western Sicily during the 2024–2025 RSV season, established to evaluate the implementation, safety, and respiratory outcomes associated with nirsevimab prophylaxis. Results: Viral–viral and/or viral–bacterial co-detections were identified in 45.5% of hospitalized infants and were associated with longer hospital stay (8.86 vs. 6.35 days, p < 0.001) and a higher frequency of complications (18.6% vs. 8.5%, p < 0.001), without differences in age, sex, or respiratory support requirements. RSV remained the predominant pathogen throughout the study period. In the complementary observational subgroup analysis, the observed safety findings were reassuring, with only mild and self-limiting events reported during follow-up; however, the sample size and observational design limit the ability to assess rare or delayed adverse events. Among infants undergoing clinically indicated respiratory testing, RSV was detected in 7/9 (77.8%) non-prophylaxed infants and 5/24 (20.8%) prophylaxed infants. Among hospitalized infants, complications were less frequently observed among those who had received nirsevimab (3.5% vs. 26.9%, p < 0.001), although substantial baseline differences between groups precluded causal interpretation of this finding. Conclusions: Viral–viral and viral–bacterial co-detections are frequent in hospitalized bronchiolitis and are associated with increased clinical complexity, mainly through prolonged hospitalization and higher occurrence of complications. The observational nirsevimab analysis showed a favorable safety profile and a lower proportion of RSV detection among clinically tested prophylaxed infants. However, because respiratory testing was performed according to clinical indication and differed substantially between groups, these findings cannot be interpreted as estimates of RSV incidence or as definitive evidence of nirsevimab effectiveness. The observed difference in complications should also be interpreted cautiously because of substantial baseline differences between prophylaxed and non-prophylaxed infants. Prospective studies with systematic respiratory testing are warranted.

Question and background
Bronchiolitis is a leading cause of hospitalization in infants, with respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) as the main etiological agent. The clinical relevance of respiratory pathogen co-infections and the role of emerging preventive strategies, including nirsevimab immunoprophylaxis, remain under investigation.

The second was a separate observational cohort of 458 newborns from two birth centers in Western Sicily during the 2024–2025 RSV season.

The second study was a separate observational cohort evaluating the implementation, safety, and respiratory outcomes associated with nirsevimab prophylaxis.

Among infants undergoing clinically indicated respiratory testing, RSV was detected in 7/9 (77.8%) non-prophylaxed infants and 5/24 (20.8%) prophylaxed infants.

单剂量佐剂 RSVPreF3 疫苗 在中国 60 岁以上成人中的免疫原性和安全性:3 期免疫桥试验结果

Immunogenicity and safety of a single dose of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine in adults ≥60 years of age in China: results from a phase 3 immunobridging trial
作者： Xiang Guo, Guijun Ning, Dan Bi, Silvia Damaso, Monique Dodet, Fang Huang, Xinji Zhang, Flavio Genco, LU Hong-mei, Jingjing Zhao
摘要中文翻译简介 呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)是严重呼吸道疾病的重要原因,但中国尚无适用于老年人的疫苗。这项 3 期、随机、部分盲法免疫桥接研究 (NCT06551181) 在 60 岁以上的中国成年人与非中国人群中比较了单剂量辅助 RSVPreF3 疫苗。方法 中国参与者以 2:1 的比例随机接受 RSVPreF3 辅助治疗(RSVPreF3_中国组)或安慰剂(Placebo_中国);来自另外六个国家(RSVPreF3_海外)的参与者收到了辅助 RSVPreF3。主要目标是证明中国参与者与非中国参与者在 疫苗接种 后 1 个月时体液 免疫应答(RSV-A/RSV-B 中和滴度)的非劣效性。如果几何平均滴度 (GMT) 比率(RSVPreF3_Overseas 与 RSVPreF3_China)的 95% 置信区间 (CI) 上限≤1.5,并且血清反应率 (SRR) 差异(RSVPreF3_Overseas 减去 RSVPreF3_China)≤10%,则满足非劣效性。还评估了反应原性和安全性。RSV-A 和 RSV-B 均满足非劣效性标准,GMT 比率的 95% CI 上限分别为 1.20 和 1.10,SRR 差异的 95% CI 上限分别为 3.40% 和 4.89%。疫苗 受者的中和滴度在 1 个月时增加:RSV-A/RSV-B GMT 在 RSVPreF3_China 组中为 5523.2/8073.2 ED60(平均几何增加 [MGI]=7.89/6.40),在 RSVPreF3_China 组中为 6604.5/8286.2 ED60(MGI=8.04/6.57)在 RSVPreF3_Overseas 组中;疫苗接种 后 6 个月时,GMT 仍高于基线。疫苗 中的请求事件比中国安慰剂接受者更频繁,但大多是轻度至中度且短暂的。疼痛(RSVPreF3_China 组的参与者为 41.7%,Placebo_China 组的参与者为 4.2%)和疲劳(13.6% 对 3.2%)分别是最常报告的给药部位事件和全身事件。主动不良事件(中国组中 11.8%–13.8% 的参与者报告)、严重不良事件(所有组中 3.4%–6.5%)和潜在免疫介导疾病(所有组中 0.2%–0.3%)的 发病率 较低且组间具有可比性。结论 单剂量含佐剂的 RSVPreF3 在 60 岁以上的中国成年人中具有免疫原性且耐受性良好,支持该人群的总体疗效和有利的获益风险概况的外推。

Immunogenicity and safety of a single dose of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine in adults ≥60 years of age in China: results from a phase 3 immunobridging trial

Authors: Xiang Guo, Guijun Ning, Dan Bi, Silvia Damaso, Monique Dodet, Fang Huang, Xinji Zhang, Flavio Genco, LU Hong-mei, Jingjing Zhao
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is an important cause of severe respiratory disease, yet no vaccine is available for older adults in China. This phase 3, randomized, partially blind immunobridging study (NCT06551181) compared a single dose of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 vaccine in ≥60-year-old Chinese adults with a non-Chinese population. Methods Chinese participants were randomized 2:1 to receive adjuvanted RSVPreF3 (RSVPreF3_China group) or placebo (Placebo_China); participants from six additional countries (RSVPreF3_Overseas) received adjuvanted RSVPreF3. The primary objective was to demonstrate non-inferiority of humoral immune responses (RSV-A/RSV-B neutralizing titers) at 1 month post-vaccination in Chinese versus non-Chinese participants. Non-inferiority was met if the upper limit of the 95% confidence interval (CI) was ≤1.5 for the geometric mean titer (GMT) ratio (RSVPreF3_Overseas over RSVPreF3_China) and ≤10% for the seroresponse rate (SRR) difference (RSVPreF3_Overseas minus RSVPreF3_China). Reactogenicity and safety were also assessed. Non-inferiority criteria were met for both RSV-A and RSV-B, with 95% CI upper limits of 1.20 and 1.10 for GMT ratios and 3.40% and 4.89% for SRR differences, respectively. Neutralizing titers increased at 1 month in vaccine recipients: RSV-A/RSV-B GMTs were 5523.2/8073.2 ED60 (mean geometric increase [MGI]=7.89/6.40) in the RSVPreF3_China group and 6604.5/8286.2 ED60 (MGI=8.04/6.57) in the RSVPreF3_Overseas group; GMTs remained above baseline at 6 months post-vaccination. Solicited events were more frequent in vaccine- than placebo-Chinese recipients but were mostly mild-to-moderate and transient. Pain (in 41.7% of participants in the RSVPreF3_China group versus 4.2% in the Placebo_China group) and fatigue (13.6% versus 3.2%) were the most frequently reported administration-site and systemic events, respectively. The incidence of unsolicited adverse events (reported in 11.8%–13.8% of participants across Chinese groups), serious adverse events (3.4%–6.5% across all groups), and potential immune-mediated diseases (0.2%–0.3% across all groups) was low and comparable between groups. Conclusion A single dose of adjuvanted RSVPreF3 was immunogenic and well tolerated in Chinese adults aged ≥60 years, supporting the extrapolation of global efficacy and a favorable benefit-risk profile in this population.

Main results

Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength.

一项评估二价 RSVpreF 疫苗 在韩国老年人中的安全性、耐受性和免疫原性的随机 3 期研究

A Randomized Phase 3 Study to Evaluate the Safety, Tolerability, and Immunogenicity of the Bivalent RSVpreF Vaccine in Older Adults in Korea
作者： Won Suk Choi, Karen Quan, James Baber, Anna Jaques, Karla Janse van Rensburg, Wen Li, Mark Cutler, Elena Kalinina, Annaliesa S. Anderson, Kena A. Swanson
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估二价 RSVpreF 疫苗 在老年人中的安全性、耐受性和免疫原性,因为其在韩国人群中的有效性数据有限。
设计与对象
这是一项针对 60 岁及以上韩国成年人的 3 期、多中心、安慰剂对照、随机 (2:1)、双盲研究。参与者接受单剂量 120 μg 的 RSVpreF 或匹配的安慰剂。
核心方法
安全终点包括 疫苗接种 后 7 天内的局部反应和全身事件以及 疫苗接种 后 1 个月内的不良事件 (AE),以及整个研究过程中的严重 AE (SAE)。疫苗接种后1个月获得RSV-A和RSV-B血清50%中和几何平均滴度(GMT)和几何平均倍数上升(GMFR)。
主要结果
Overall, 377 participants received study intervention (RSVpreF, n = 251; placebo, n = 126). Most local reactions and all systemic events were of mild or moderate severity. Injection-site pain was the most frequently reported local reaction (RSVpreF, 12.4%; placebo, 3.2%). The most frequently reported systemic events were fatigue (RSVpreF, 23.1%; placebo, 26.2%) and muscle pain (RSVpreF, 15.5%; placebo, 9.5%).
解释与创新
作者得出结论,RSVpreF 具有可接受的安全性和耐受性,并在 疫苗接种 后 1 个月引发了强劲的 RSV 中和反应,与关键的 3 期疗效试验结果一致。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果提供了有关 RSVpreF 在韩国老年人中的安全性和免疫原性的重要数据,可以为未来 疫苗 的开发和该人群 RSV 预防的公共卫生策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单次 120 μg 剂量和较短的随访期,但证据充足,因为它基于一项控制良好的随机试验,具有明确的安全性和免疫原性评估。

A Randomized Phase 3 Study to Evaluate the Safety, Tolerability, and Immunogenicity of the Bivalent RSVpreF Vaccine in Older Adults in Korea

Authors: Won Suk Choi, Karen Quan, James Baber, Anna Jaques, Karla Janse van Rensburg, Wen Li, Mark Cutler, Elena Kalinina, Annaliesa S. Anderson, Kena A. Swanson
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: The bivalent RSVpreF vaccine is effective at preventing respiratory syncytial virus (RSV)-associated lower respiratory tract illness in adults. However, data regarding safety and immunogenicity of RSVpreF in Korean populations are limited. Methods: This was a phase 3, multicenter, placebo-controlled, randomized (2:1), double-blind study in Korean adults 60 years of age and older. Participants received a single 120-μg dose of RSVpreF or matching placebo. Safety endpoints included local reactions and systemic events through 7 days and adverse events (AEs) through 1 month after vaccination, and serious AEs (SAEs) throughout the study. RSV-A and RSV-B serum 50% neutralizing geometric mean titers (GMTs) and geometric mean fold rises (GMFRs) were obtained 1 month after vaccination. Results: Overall, 377 participants received study intervention (RSVpreF, n = 251; placebo, n = 126). Most local reactions and all systemic events were of mild or moderate severity. Injection-site pain was the most frequently reported local reaction (RSVpreF, 12.4%; placebo, 3.2%). The most frequently reported systemic events were fatigue (RSVpreF, 23.1%; placebo, 26.2%) and muscle pain (RSVpreF, 15.5%; placebo, 9.5%). AEs through 1 month after vaccination were infrequent (RSVpreF, 3.6%; placebo, 0.8%); none were considered vaccine related by the investigator. RSV-A and RSV-B neutralizing GMTs increased 1 month after RSVpreF, with GMFRs (95% CIs) from before to 1 month after vaccination of 9.5 (8.51–10.67) for RSV-A and 8.3 (7.37–9.39) for RSV-B. Conclusions: In older Korean adults, RSVpreF had an acceptable safety and tolerability profile and elicited robust RSV neutralizing responses 1 month after vaccination consistent with pivotal phase 3 efficacy trial results. ClincalTrials.gov Identifier: NCT06593587 (date of registration: 9 September 2024).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the safety, tolerability, and immunogenicity of the bivalent RSVpreF vaccine in older adults, as data on its effectiveness in Korean populations are limited.
Design and population
This was a phase 3, multicenter, placebo-controlled, randomized (2:1), double-blind study in Korean adults 60 years of age and older. Participants received a single 120-μg dose of RSVpreF or matching placebo.
Core methods
Safety endpoints included local reactions and systemic events through 7 days and adverse events (AEs) through 1 month after vaccination, and serious AEs (SAEs) throughout the study. RSV-A and RSV-B serum 50% neutralizing geometric mean titers (GMTs) and geometric mean fold rises (GMFRs) were obtained 1 month after vaccination.
Main results
Overall, 377 participants received study intervention (RSVpreF, n = 251; placebo, n = 126). Most local reactions and all systemic events were of mild or moderate severity. Injection-site pain was the most frequently reported local reaction (RSVpreF, 12.4%; placebo, 3.2%). The most frequently reported systemic events were fatigue (RSVpreF, 23.1%; placebo, 26.2%) and muscle pain (RSVpreF, 15.5%; placebo, 9.5%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that RSVpreF had an acceptable safety and tolerability profile and elicited robust RSV neutralizing responses 1 month after vaccination consistent with pivotal phase 3 efficacy trial results.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide important data on the safety and immunogenicity of RSVpreF in older Korean adults, which can inform future vaccine development and public health strategies for RSV prevention in this population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single 120-μg dose and a short follow-up period, but the evidence is strong as it is based on a well-controlled, randomized trial with a clear safety and immunogenicity assessment.

肺部先天淋巴细胞驱动的血清素能神经免疫轴协调气道高反应性和咳嗽过敏性。

A pulmonary innate lymphoid cell-driven serotonergic neuroimmune axis orchestrates airway hyperreactivity and cough hypersensitivity.
作者： Chia-Hui Luo, Jheng-Syuan Shao, Alan Chuan-Ying Lai, Wei-Chang Huang, Ko-Chien Wu, Wei-Yu Chen, Chih-Cheng Chen, Nicholas W Lukacs, Ya-Jen Chang
摘要中文翻译哮喘是一种异质性疾病,其特征是气道高反应性 (AHR) 和慢性咳嗽,并因呼吸道病毒或过敏原暴露而加剧。然而,这些反应背后的免疫途径介导的血清素能回路仍然不明确。在这里,我们确定了一个血清素能神经免疫轴,其中肺组 2 先天淋巴细胞 (ILC2) 表达色氨酸羟化酶-1 (Tph1),并在白细胞介素 (IL)-33 刺激后通过 CCAAT/增强子结合蛋白 β (C/EBPβ) 依赖性转录释放血清素。ILC2 中的 Tph1 缺陷和过继转移实验表明,ILC2 衍生的血清素通过 5-羟色胺 (5-HT)2A-NF-κB 激活放大 2 型细胞因子的产生,导致 呼吸道合胞病毒 驱动的哮喘模型中 AHR 加剧。使用工程病毒工具,我们发现 ILC2 衍生的血清素通过 5-HT3A 使迷走神经感觉神经元敏感,从而促进咳嗽过敏。临床上,哮喘患者表现出 TPH1 和血浆血清素水平升高,这两者都与 2 型细胞因子相关。总之,这些发现揭示了 ILC2 驱动的血清素能回路有助于哮喘病理生理学,并强调血清素能信号作为治疗靶点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在了解呼吸道病毒或过敏原导致哮喘恶化的免疫途径,特别关注导致气道高反应性和咳嗽过敏的血清素能回路。
设计与对象
该研究利用 呼吸道合胞病毒 驱动的哮喘体内模型并进行过继转移实验,以研究肺部 2 型先天淋巴细胞 (ILC2) 在血清素能信号传导中的作用。
核心方法
该研究采用工程病毒工具和分子检测来证明 ILC2 表达色氨酸羟化酶-1 (Tph1) 并释放血清素,并分析其对细胞因子产生和气道高反应性的影响。
主要结果
ILC2 中的 Tph1 缺陷减少了血清素的释放,过继转移实验表明,ILC2 衍生的血清素通过 5-HT2A-NF-κB 激活放大了 2 型细胞因子的产生,导致 RSV 驱动的哮喘模型中气道高反应性加剧。
解释与创新
研究结果揭示了一种由 ILC2 驱动的新型血清素能神经免疫轴,有助于哮喘病理生理学,强调血清素作为潜在的治疗靶点。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 呼吸道合胞病毒 哮喘恶化机制的见解,并表明血清素信号传导是开发靶向治疗的一个有前途的途径。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于体内模型,不包括临床试验,但由于在 RSV 驱动的模型中 ILC2 衍生的血清素与哮喘症状之间存在直接机制联系,因此证据充足。

A pulmonary innate lymphoid cell-driven serotonergic neuroimmune axis orchestrates airway hyperreactivity and cough hypersensitivity.

Authors: Chia-Hui Luo, Jheng-Syuan Shao, Alan Chuan-Ying Lai, Wei-Chang Huang, Ko-Chien Wu, Wei-Yu Chen, Chih-Cheng Chen, Nicholas W Lukacs, Ya-Jen Chang
Original abstract (English)
Asthma is a heterogeneous disease characterized by airway hyperreactivity (AHR) and chronic cough, exacerbated by respiratory viruses or allergen exposure. Yet, the immune pathway-mediated serotonergic circuits underlying these responses remain poorly defined. Here, we identified a serotonergic neuroimmune axis in which pulmonary group 2 innate lymphoid cells (ILC2s) expressed tryptophan hydroxylase-1 (Tph1) and released serotonin via CCAAT/enhancer-binding protein beta (C/EBPβ)-dependent transcription following interleukin (IL)-33 stimulation. Tph1 deficiency in ILC2s and adoptive transfer experiments demonstrated that ILC2-derived serotonin amplified type 2 cytokine production via 5-hydroxytryptamine (5-HT)2A-NF-κB activation, resulting in exacerbated AHR in respiratory syncytial virus-driven asthmatic models. Using engineered viral tools, we showed that ILC2-derived serotonin sensitized vagal sensory neurons through 5-HT3A to promote cough hypersensitivity. Clinically, asthma patients exhibited elevated TPH1 and plasma serotonin levels, both of which correlated with type 2 cytokines. Together, these findings reveal that an ILC2-driven serotonergic circuit contributes to asthma pathophysiology and highlight serotonergic signaling as a therapeutic target.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the immune pathways involved in asthma exacerbation by respiratory viruses or allergens, particularly focusing on the serotonergic circuits that contribute to airway hyperreactivity and cough hypersensitivity.
Design and population
The research utilized in vivo models of respiratory syncytial virus-driven asthma and conducted adoptive transfer experiments to investigate the role of pulmonary group 2 innate lymphoid cells (ILC2s) in serotonergic signaling.
Core methods
The study employed engineered viral tools and molecular assays to demonstrate that ILC2s express tryptophan hydroxylase-1 (Tph1) and release serotonin, which was analyzed for its effects on cytokine production and airway hyperreactivity.
Main results
Tph1 deficiency in ILC2s reduced serotonin release, and adoptive transfer experiments showed that ILC2-derived serotonin amplified type 2 cytokine production via 5-HT2A-NF-κB activation, leading to exacerbated airway hyperreactivity in RSV-driven asthmatic models.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a novel serotonergic neuroimmune axis driven by ILC2s that contributes to asthma pathophysiology, highlighting serotonin as a potential therapeutic target.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms of asthma exacerbation by respiratory syncytial virus and suggest serotonergic signaling as a promising avenue for developing targeted therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vivo models and does not include clinical trials, but the evidence is strong due to the direct mechanistic link between ILC2-derived serotonin and asthma symptoms in RSV-driven models.

Comparative epidemiology of six respiratory pathogens among children aged 28 days to 14 years with acute respiratory infections in coastal Xiamen and inland Linxia, China: a multicenter retrospective study

Authors: Xingxiang Zheng, Hongmei Ma, Jiali Cao, Jumei Liu, Rui Guo, Qunshan Xu, Xiaoli Chen, Shiqiang Han, Huiming Ye
Original abstract (English)
Objective Acute respiratory infections are a major public health problem in children. The epidemiology of pediatric respiratory pathogens has shifted in recent years. This study aimed to compare the epidemiological patterns of six common respiratory pathogens among children with acute respiratory infections (ARI) in Xiamen and Linxia, China. Methods This retrospective study included 5,835 children with ARI from December 2024 to November 2025. Oropharyngeal swabs were tested for rhinovirus (RV), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza A virus (Flu A), influenza B virus (Flu B), adenovirus (ADV), and Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) using multiplex RT-PCR. Detection rates were compared by region, sex, age group and season. Results Overall, 3,143 samples (53.9%) were positive; 290 (5.0%) had co-detections. RV (23.8%), RSV (20.2%) and Flu A (8.8%) were most frequently detected. Positivity was higher in Linxia than in Xiamen (65.4% vs. After adjustment, Linxia remained associated with higher odds of detection (aOR 1.86, 95% CI 1.65–2.10). Detection was also associated with male sex, toddler/preschool age and autumn/winter seasons. Conclusion RV, RSV and Flu A were the most frequently detected pathogens in both regions. The findings indicate regional, age-related and seasonal heterogeneity, supporting locally tailored surveillance and prevention planning.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Acute respiratory infections are a major public health problem in children. This study aimed to compare the epidemiological patterns of six common respiratory pathogens among children with acute respiratory infections (ARI) in Xiamen and Linxia, China.
Design and population
This multicenter retrospective study included 5,835 children with ARI from December 2024 to November 2025. The study was conducted in coastal Xiamen and inland Linxia, China.
Core methods
Oropharyngeal swabs were tested for rhinovirus (RV), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), influenza A virus (Flu A), influenza B virus (Flu B), adenovirus (ADV), and Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP) using multiplex RT-PCR. Detection rates were compared by region, sex, age group and season.
Main results
Overall, 3,143 samples (53.9%) were positive; 290 (5.0%) had co-detections. Positivity was higher in Linxia than in Xiamen (65.4% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate regional, age-related and seasonal heterogeneity, supporting locally tailored surveillance and prevention planning.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study highlights the importance of understanding regional variations in respiratory pathogen detection to inform targeted surveillance and prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a retrospective design and may be subject to biases inherent in such studies. The evidence is moderate as it is based on a single study with potential limitations in data collection and interpretation.

治疗还是观察?治疗患有复杂疾病的患者的嵌合分枝杆菌

Treat or observe? Managing Mycobacterium Chimaera in a patient with complex morbidties
作者： Authors unavailable
摘要中文翻译在缺乏此类发现的情况下,阳性培养可能代表定植。进行了支气管肺泡灌洗以评估感染性病因,患者在结果待出时被出院。感染性疾病会诊认为由于缺乏典型NTM肺病的放射学发现,如空洞或结节性浸润,不应开始治疗。讨论:分离出M. chimaera并不一定意味着活动性感染,因为NTM肺病的诊断需要与临床症状和特征性放射学异常相符合。本病例强调了整合临床、微生物学和放射学数据的重要性,并突出了多学科决策在避免对复杂合并症和有限预后的患者进行不必要的长期治疗中的作用。64引言Mycobacterium chimaera是一种非结核分枝杆菌(NTM),是一种新兴病原体,给诊断和治疗带来挑战,尤其是在患有基础肺病和复杂病史的患者中。当存在多种呼吸症状的替代解释时,区分真正的感染与定植往往困难。病例描述一名61岁男性,有滤泡性淋巴瘤转化为弥漫大B细胞淋巴瘤的病史,曾接受化疗、放疗和自体骨髓移植治疗,最近被诊断为小细胞肺癌,出现进行性呼吸困难。他的症状被归因于多种病因,包括与durvalumab相关的肺炎,用糖皮质激素治疗;呼吸道合胞病毒感染合并细菌性肺炎,...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决嵌合分枝杆菌对具有复杂病史和潜在肺部疾病的患者带来的诊断和治疗挑战。这一背景凸显了在这种情况下区分真正的感染和定植的困难。
设计与对象
本病例研究描述了一名 61 岁男性,有滤泡性淋巴瘤、弥漫性大 B 细胞淋巴瘤和小细胞肺癌病史,并出现进行性呼吸困难。该研究涉及临床、微生物学和放射学评估以及多学科决策。
核心方法
患者接受了支气管肺泡灌洗(BAL)以评估感染原因,结果用于指导临床决策。多学科讨论包括传染病、肿瘤学、肺部和姑息治疗团队,以确定适当的管理策略。
主要结果
BAL 结果显示嵌合分枝杆菌呈阳性,但没有出现 NTM 肺部疾病典型的影像学表现,例如空洞或结节性浸润。该患者的呼吸道症状有多种替代解释,包括杜瓦鲁单抗相关肺炎、RSV 感染和细菌性肺炎。
解释与创新
作者解释说,分离出嵌合分枝杆菌并不一定表明存在主动感染,尤其是在存在其他解释的情况下。该病例强调了整合临床和放射学数据以避免不必要的长期治疗的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,需要仔细解释患有复杂合并症和呼吸道症状多种潜在原因的患者的嵌合分枝杆菌培养物,特别是对于那些预后有限的患者。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个病例,不提供统计证据或更广泛的人群数据。这些证据基于临床判断和现有的诊断结果,可能无法概括。研究结果的可信度为中等。

Treat or observe? Managing Mycobacterium Chimaera in a patient with complex morbidties

Authors: Authors unavailable
Original abstract (English)
In the absence of such findings, positive cultures may represent colonization. Bronchoalveloar lavage was performed to evaluate infectious causes, and the patient was discharged while results were pending. Infectious disease consultation recommended against initiating therapy due to the absence of radiographic findings typical of NTM pulmonary disease, such as cavitation or nodular infiltrates, and the presence of multiple alternative explanations for his respiratory symptoms. DiscussionIsolation of M. chimaera does not necessarily indicate active infection, as diagnosis of NTM pulmonary disease requires compatible clinical symptoms and characteristic radiographic abnormalities. This case highlights the importance of integrating clinical, microbiologic, and radiographic data and emphasizes the role of multidisciplinary decision-making to avoid unnecessary prolonged therapy in patients with complex comorbidities and limited prognosis. 64IntroductionMycobacterium chimaera, a non-tuberculous mycobacterium (NTM), is an emerging pathogen that presents diagnostic and therapeutic challenges, particularly in patients with underlying pulmonary disease and complex medical histories. Distinguishing true infection from colonization is often difficult when alternative explanations for respiratory symptoms are present.Case DescriptionA 61-year-old man with a history of follicular lymphoma transformed to diffuse large B-cell lymphoma, previously treated with chemotherapy, radiation, and autologous bone marrow transplantation, and a recent diagnosis of small cell lung cancer, presented with progressive dyspnea. His symptoms were attributed to multiple etiologies, including durvalumab-related pneumonitis treated with corticosteroids, RSV infection with superimposed bacterial pneumonia,...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the diagnostic and therapeutic challenges posed by Mycobacterium chimaera in patients with complex medical histories and underlying pulmonary disease. The background highlights the difficulty in distinguishing true infection from colonization in such cases.
Design and population
This case study describes a 61-year-old man with a history of follicular lymphoma, diffuse large B-cell lymphoma, and small cell lung cancer who presented with progressive dyspnea. The study involved clinical, microbiologic, and radiographic evaluations, as well as multidisciplinary decision-making.
Core methods
The patient underwent bronchoalveolar lavage (BAL) to evaluate infectious causes, and the results were used to inform clinical decisions. Multidisciplinary discussions included infectious disease, oncology, pulmonary, and palliative care teams to determine the appropriate management strategy.
Main results
The BAL results returned positive for Mycobacterium chimaera, but there were no radiographic findings typical of NTM pulmonary disease, such as cavitation or nodular infiltrates. The patient had multiple alternative explanations for his respiratory symptoms, including durvalumab-related pneumonitis, RSV infection, and bacterial pneumonia.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the isolation of M. chimaera does not necessarily indicate active infection, especially in the presence of alternative explanations. This case emphasizes the importance of integrating clinical and radiographic data to avoid unnecessary prolonged therapy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for careful interpretation of M. chimaera cultures in patients with complex comorbidities and multiple potential causes of respiratory symptoms, particularly in those with limited prognosis.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single case and does not provide statistical evidence or broader population data. The evidence is based on clinical judgment and available diagnostic results, which may not be generalizable. The confidence in the findings is moderate.

Clesrovimab,一种用于预防婴儿 呼吸道合胞病毒 病的单克隆抗体:药物概况。

Clesrovimab, a monoclonal antibody for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus disease in infants: a drug profile.
作者： Margarethe Wiedenmann, Ziyaad Dangor, Shabir A. Madhi

范围与问题
我们综合了 1 期至 3 期临床试验的证据、药代动力学和药效学数据、监管批准和耐药性分析,借鉴了截至 2026 年 5 月的同行评审文献和监管资源。

共识与结论

Clesrovimab, a monoclonal antibody for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus disease in infants: a drug profile.

Authors: Margarethe Wiedenmann, Ziyaad Dangor, Shabir A. Madhi
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of acute lower respiratory tract infection (LRTI) in young children. In children < 5 years old, it causes an estimated 33 million LRTI episodes and over 100,000 deaths annually, two-thirds of which occur in infants. The licensure of new long-acting monoclonal antibodies and a maternal RSV vaccine offer opportunities for reducing RSV related infant morbidity and mortality. AREAS COVERED: This drug profile reviews clesrovimab (Enflonsia, Merck & Co. Rahway, NJ U.S.A.), an extended half-life human IgG1κ monoclonal antibody, targeting the highly conserved antigenic site IV of the RSV fusion protein. We synthesize evidence from phase 1 to phase 3 clinical trials, pharmacokinetic and pharmacodynamic data, regulatory approvals and resistance profiling, drawing on peer-reviewed literature and regulatory sources available until May 2026. EXPERT OPINION: Clesrovimab offers a single, fixed-dose, weight-independent injection per RSV season, with demonstrated safety and efficacy against medically attended RSV disease and hospitalization in infants. Its epitope target is distinct from that of nirsevimab (site Ø) and no cross resistance between the two antibodies has been observed in vitro. Equitable global access to monoclonal antibodies, particularly for low- and middle-income countries, remains critical to reduce the global RSV burden.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
We synthesize evidence from phase 1 to phase 3 clinical trials, pharmacokinetic and pharmacodynamic data, regulatory approvals and resistance profiling, drawing on peer-reviewed literature and regulatory sources available until May 2026.
Evidence base and method
The supplied evidence does not clearly state the review method, databases, eligibility process, or evidence base.
Consensus and conclusions
EXPERT OPINION: Clesrovimab offers a single, fixed-dose, weight-independent injection per RSV season, with demonstrated safety and efficacy against medically attended RSV disease and hospitalization in infants.
Controversies and gaps
The supplied evidence does not clearly state controversies or evidence gaps.
Research and practice implications
The supplied evidence does not clearly state a specific research or practice implication.

呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)在欧洲实施新的RSV预防策略之前住院儿童中的流行情况:一项系统综述和荟萃分析。

The epidemiology of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in hospitalized children in Europe before the widespread implementation of new RSV prophylactic strategies: A systematic review and meta-analysis.
作者： Maria M. Berikopoulou, Danai K. Giavasoglou, Argyro Konstantopoulou, Ilias Gountas, Tania Siahanidou, Athanasios Michos
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 是小儿呼吸道感染的主要原因。这项系统回顾和荟萃分析旨在评估欧洲儿童在 COVID-19 之后和实施近期 RSV 预防策略之前的院内阳性率。
证据基础与方法
A systematic search was conducted across three databases: PubMed, Scopus and Web of Science from 01/01/2021-31/12/2025. A random-effect model was applied and heterogeneity was assessed using I 2 statistics.
共识与结论
院内汇总 RSV 总体阳性率为 48%(95%CI:41%-56%)。每年的阳性估计为:2021-2022 年为 53% (95%CI:49%-56%),2022-2023 年为 49% (95%CI:36%-62%),2023-2024 年为 62% (95%CI:51%-73%) (p = 0.308)。在研究期间,RSV-B 比 RSV-A 占优势。
争议与缺口
在针对婴儿(<12 个月大)的研究中,RSV 阳性率为 69%,而在针对较大儿童(0-18 岁)的研究中,RSV 阳性率为 30%。检测到与 RSV 相关的住院治疗造成很大负担,特别是对于 12 个月以下的婴儿;然而,应谨慎解释总体汇总阳性估计值。
科研与实践启示
这些发现为评估母体 RSV 疫苗接种 和单克隆抗体的影响提供了重要的免疫前基线,并支持在欧洲继续使用 RSV 监测 的需要。

The epidemiology of respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in hospitalized children in Europe before the widespread implementation of new RSV prophylactic strategies: A systematic review and meta-analysis.

Authors: Maria M. Berikopoulou, Danai K. Giavasoglou, Argyro Konstantopoulou, Ilias Gountas, Tania Siahanidou, Athanasios Michos
Original abstract (English)
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of pediatric respiratory infections. This systematic review and meta-analysis aims to estimate the in-hospital positivity among children in Europe post-COVID-19 and prior to the implementation of the recent RSV prophylactic strategies. A systematic search was conducted across three databases: PubMed, Scopus and Web of Science from 01/01/2021-31/12/2025. Studies enrolling children aged 0-18 years who were hospitalized with respiratory infection and detected positive for RSV were eligible for inclusion. A random-effect model was applied and heterogeneity was assessed using I 2 statistics. Thirty-eight studies met the inclusion criteria, encompasing 66,065 children. The overall pooled RSV in-hospital positivity was 48% (95%CI: 41%-56%). Positivity estimates per year were: 53% (95%CI:49%-56%) for 2021-2022, 49% (95%CI:36%-62%) for 2022-2023, and 62% (95%CI:51%-73%) for 2023-2024 (p = 0.308). RSV positivity in studies with infants (<12-months-old) was 69% vs 30% in studies including older children (0-18 years). Among RSV-positive patients, co-infections were detected in 17%, respiratory support was required in 62% and ICU/NICU admission in 11%. RSV-B predominated over RSV-A during the study period. A substantial burden of RSV-related hospitalizations was detected, especially in infants < 12-months-old; however, the overall pooled positivity estimate should be interpreted with caution. These findings provide an important pre-immunization baseline for evaluating the impact of maternal RSV vaccination and monoclonal antibodies and support the need for continued RSV surveillance across Europe.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) is a leading cause of pediatric respiratory infections. This systematic review and meta-analysis aims to estimate the in-hospital positivity among children in Europe post-COVID-19 and prior to the implementation of the recent RSV prophylactic strategies.
Evidence base and method
A systematic search was conducted across three databases: PubMed, Scopus and Web of Science from 01/01/2021-31/12/2025. A random-effect model was applied and heterogeneity was assessed using I 2 statistics.
Consensus and conclusions
The overall pooled RSV in-hospital positivity was 48% (95%CI: 41%-56%). Positivity estimates per year were: 53% (95%CI:49%-56%) for 2021-2022, 49% (95%CI:36%-62%) for 2022-2023, and 62% (95%CI:51%-73%) for 2023-2024 (p = 0.308). RSV-B predominated over RSV-A during the study period.
Controversies and gaps
RSV positivity in studies with infants (<12-months-old) was 69% vs 30% in studies including older children (0-18 years). A substantial burden of RSV-related hospitalizations was detected, especially in infants < 12-months-old; however, the overall pooled positivity estimate should be interpreted with caution.
Research and practice implications
These findings provide an important pre-immunization baseline for evaluating the impact of maternal RSV vaccination and monoclonal antibodies and support the need for continued RSV surveillance across Europe.

尼塞韦单抗 用于 呼吸道合胞病毒 预防:全面的产品审查。

Nirsevimab for respiratory syncytial virus prevention: A comprehensive product review.
作者： Josefina L. Razzini, Federico Martinon-Torres, Federico Martinón‐Torres
摘要中文翻译婴儿 RSV 预防正在经历公共卫生模式向可扩展、广泛适用的被动免疫的转变。本综述研究了 尼塞韦单抗 (Beyfortus®),这是一种长效重组 IgG1κ 单克隆抗体,靶向融合前 RSV F 蛋白的保守位点 Ø。其半衰期延长,单剂量即可提供整个季节的保护,克服了帕利珠单抗的主要局限性。经过验证的制造平台和严格的发布规范确保了一致的质量和效力。关键试验证明了对 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染住院 (RSVH) 的高效治疗,而务实的 HARMONIE 试验证实,在常规条件下,RSVH 减少了 83.2%。多个国家的许可后研究表明,针对 RSVH 的有效性为 70-90%,并且重症监护室入院人数大幅减少。2024-2025 年的比较数据进一步表明,比母体 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 具有更好的保护作用。尼塞韦单抗 代表了一种可扩展的公共卫生干预措施,可以单独使用或补充母体 RSV 疫苗接种,保证 监测 的持续有效性、安全性和病毒逃逸。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
该综述的重点是 呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 预防,特别研究 呼吸道合胞病毒 作为减少婴儿 RSV 相关下呼吸道感染住院 (RSVH) 的潜在干预措施。核心问题是 尼塞韦单抗 是否可以作为 RSV 预防的有效且可扩展的被动免疫策略。
证据基础与方法
该审查是对 尼塞韦单抗 的全面产品审查,尼塞韦单抗 是一种长效重组 IgG1κ 单克隆抗体,针对融合前 RSV F 蛋白的保守位点 Ø。它包括关键试验和务实的 HARMONIE 试验,用于评估常规条件下的疗效和有效性。该综述没有明确描述荟萃分析或共识过程,但其研究结果依赖于这些试验。
共识与结论
关键试验证明了对 RSVH 的高效功效,务实的 HARMONIE 试验证实在常规条件下 RSVH 减少了 83.2%。多个国家的许可后研究表明,针对 RSVH 的有效性为 70-90%,并且重症监护室入院人数大幅减少。这些发现表明 尼塞韦单抗 是一种有前景的 RSV 预防干预措施。
争议与缺口
该审查指出,2024-2025 年的比较数据表明,比母体 RSVpreF 疫苗接种 提供了更大的保护,但没有详细说明造成这种差异的具体原因。此外,该审查强调需要继续 监测 的有效性、安全性和病毒逃逸,表明有关长期结果和潜在耐药性的尚未解决的问题。
科研与实践启示
尼塞韦单抗 代表了一种可扩展的公共卫生干预措施,可以单独使用或与母体 RSV 疫苗接种 结合使用。该审查表明,需要进一步研究来监测其有效性、安全性和病毒逃逸的可能性,并探索其在不同人群和地区的使用。

Nirsevimab for respiratory syncytial virus prevention: A comprehensive product review.

Authors: Josefina L. Razzini, Federico Martinon-Torres, Federico Martinón‐Torres
Original abstract (English)
Infant RSV prevention is undergoing a public-health paradigm shift toward scalable, broad-eligibility passive immunization. This review examines nirsevimab (Beyfortus®), a long-acting recombinant IgG1κ monoclonal antibody targeting the conserved site Ø of prefusion RSV F protein. Its extended half-life enables season-long protection with a single dose, overcoming key limitations of palivizumab. A validated manufacturing platform and stringent release specifications ensure consistent quality and potency. Pivotal trials demonstrated high efficacy against RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection hospitalization (RSVH), while the pragmatic HARMONIE trial confirmed an 83.2% reduction in RSVH under routine conditions. Postlicensure studies across multiple countries report 70-90% effectiveness against RSVH and substantial reductions in intensive care admissions. Comparative 2024-2025 data further suggest greater protection than maternal RSVpreF vaccination. Nirsevimab represents a scalable public-health intervention that can be used alone or complement maternal RSV vaccination, warranting continued surveillance of effectiveness, safety, and viral escape.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) prevention, specifically examining nirsevimab as a potential intervention for reducing RSV-associated lower respiratory tract infection hospitalization (RSVH) in infants. The central question is whether nirsevimab can serve as an effective and scalable passive immunization strategy for RSV prevention.
Evidence base and method
The review is a comprehensive product review of nirsevimab, a long-acting recombinant IgG1κ monoclonal antibody targeting the conserved site Ø of the prefusion RSV F protein. It includes pivotal trials and a pragmatic HARMONIE trial, which were used to assess efficacy and effectiveness under routine conditions. The review does not explicitly describe the meta-analysis or consensus process, but it relies on these trials for its findings.
Consensus and conclusions
Pivotal trials demonstrated high efficacy against RSVH, and the pragmatic HARMONIE trial confirmed an 83.2% reduction in RSVH under routine conditions. Postlicensure studies across multiple countries report 70-90% effectiveness against RSVH and substantial reductions in intensive care admissions. These findings suggest that nirsevimab is a promising intervention for RSV prevention.
Controversies and gaps
The review notes that comparative 2024-2025 data suggest greater protection than maternal RSVpreF vaccination, but it does not elaborate on the specific reasons for this discrepancy. Additionally, the review highlights the need for continued surveillance of effectiveness, safety, and viral escape, indicating unresolved questions about long-term outcomes and potential resistance.
Research and practice implications
Nirsevimab represents a scalable public-health intervention that can be used alone or in combination with maternal RSV vaccination. The review suggests that further research is needed to monitor its effectiveness, safety, and potential for viral escape, as well as to explore its use in different populations and regions.

呼吸道合胞病毒疾病在高收入国家成人社区和初级医疗环境中的负担:一项系统综述、荟萃分析和建模研究

Respiratory syncytial virus disease burden among adults in community and primary care settings in high-income countries: a systematic review, meta-analysis, and modelling study
作者： You Li, Sebastien Kenmoe, Elizabeth Begier, Pia Wahi-Singh, Bhanu Wahi-Singh, Caihua Liang, Reiko Sato, Bradford D. Gessner, Harish Nair
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本研究旨在评估高收入国家成年人社区和初级保健机构中与 RSV 相关的急性呼吸道感染 (ARI) 疾病负担。重点是不同年龄组和环境中的 发病率 率和 RSV 阳性比例。
证据基础与方法
我们进行了系统性文献综述(PROSPERO 注册号:CRD42022377741),以确定已发表的关于高收入国家成人社区或初级保健机构中 RSV 发病率和 死亡率 负担的研究。随机效应荟萃分析用于综合各个研究的估计值。共有六项研究对 RSV 发病率 率估计做出了贡献,并通过对报告的 发病率 率和阳性比例估计值应用校正因子来解决潜在病例的确定不足问题。
共识与结论
该研究揭示了高收入国家所有年龄段成年人的社区和初级保健机构都面临着巨大的 RSV 疾病负担。社区中 18-49 岁和 50 岁以上人群的调整后 RSV 发病率 率相似。在门诊环境中,调整后的 RSV 发病率 率随着年龄的增长而增加,范围从 18-49 岁成人的 2231/100,000 人年到 65 岁以上成人的 4079/100,000 人年。在社区、门诊和急诊室中,成人 ARI 患者中调整后的 RSV 阳性比例总体相似(范围:3.7-5.1%)。
争议与缺口
该研究承认,由于呼吸道标本和诊断测试的测试敏感性不完善,RSV 病例的确定可能存在不足。关于社区和初级保健机构(尤其是老年人)RSV 疾病负担的数据有限,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。
科研与实践启示
这些发现强调了在预测 RSV 疫苗接种 的潜在影响时考虑社区和初级保健机构中 RSV 疾病负担的重要性。需要进一步的研究来更好地了解 RSV 在不同人群和环境中的负担,特别是在老年人中。

Respiratory syncytial virus disease burden among adults in community and primary care settings in high-income countries: a systematic review, meta-analysis, and modelling study

Authors: You Li, Sebastien Kenmoe, Elizabeth Begier, Pia Wahi-Singh, Bhanu Wahi-Singh, Caihua Liang, Reiko Sato, Bradford D. Gessner, Harish Nair
Original abstract (English)
In this study, we aimed to estimate RSV-associated acute respiratory infection (ARI) disease burden in community and primary-care settings among adults in high-income countries. We conducted a systematic literature review (PROSPERO registration: CRD42022377741) to identify published studies on RSV morbidity and mortality burden in community (including ill adults seeking or not seeking health care identified by prospective cohort studies with active case ascertainment) or primary-care settings (including outpatient and emergency department, ED) in adults in high-income countries. The adjusted RSV incidence rate in the community was similar between persons aged 18–49 years (6104/100,000 person-years, 95% CI: 3737–9971; 1 study) and ≥ 50 years (5311/100,000 person-years, 95% CI: 3690–7643; 2 studies). Based on 1 study, RSV incidence rate in the ED was comparable between those aged 18–49 years (309/100,000, 142–676) and 50–64 years (300/100,000, 98–919), and was higher in adults aged ≥65 years (685/100,000 person-years, 95% CI: 258–2058). This study revealed substantial RSV disease burden in community and primary-care settings across all age strata of adults in high-income countries. Abstract Existing reports of RSV disease burden in adults focus primarily on hospitalisation while little is known about RSV disease burden in community and primary-care settings. We accounted for the potential case under-ascertainment of RSV related to the imperfect test sensitivity of respiratory specimens and diagnostic tests by applying correction factors to the reported incidence rate and positive proportion estimates in individual studies. We estimated incidence rate and RSV positive proportion among those tested with acute respiratory illness by age group and setting. Random-effects met...
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
This study aimed to estimate RSV-associated acute respiratory infection (ARI) disease burden in community and primary-care settings among adults in high-income countries. The focus was on incidence rates and RSV positive proportion across different age groups and settings.
Evidence base and method
A systematic literature review (PROSPERO registration: CRD42022377741) was conducted to identify published studies on RSV morbidity and mortality burden in community or primary-care settings in adults in high-income countries. Random-effects meta-analysis was used to synthesise the estimates across studies. A total of six studies contributed to RSV incidence rate estimates, and the potential case under-ascertainment was addressed by applying correction factors to the reported incidence rate and positive proportion estimates.
Consensus and conclusions
The study revealed substantial RSV disease burden in community and primary-care settings across all age strata of adults in high-income countries. The adjusted RSV incidence rate in the community was similar between persons aged 18–49 years and ≥ 50 years. In the outpatient setting, the adjusted RSV incidence rate increased with age, ranging from 2231/100,000 person-years in adults aged 18–49 years to 4079/100,000 person-years in adults aged ≥ 65 years. The adjusted RSV positive proportion in adults with ARI was generally similar across the community, outpatient, and ED settings (range: 3.7–5.1%).
Controversies and gaps
The study acknowledges the potential under-ascertainment of RSV cases due to the imperfect test sensitivity of respiratory specimens and diagnostic tests. There is limited data on RSV disease burden in community and primary-care settings, particularly for older adults, which may affect the generalisability of the findings.
Research and practice implications
These findings highlight the importance of considering RSV disease burden in community and primary-care settings when anticipating the potential impact of RSV vaccination. Further research is needed to better understand the burden of RSV in different populations and settings, especially in older adults.
